867 entries, retro to retrospective. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **retro**: Look back at a sprint that has landed - read its spec, its stories, its wave logs and its review, move what should outlive it into the feature list, the glossary, an ADR, the standards and the stack skills, propose a check for every mistake a tool could have caught, and move its folder to done.
